1       Summary of Performance
1.A     Introduction
This Budget Implementation Report for Kano State is prepared quarterly and issued within 30 days from the
end of each quarter.

This report includes the original approved budget and revised/final budget appropriations for the year 2025
against each organisational unit for each of the core economic classification of expenditures (Personnel,
Overheads, Capital, and Others); the actual expenditures for the quarter Q2, attributed to each
organisational unit, as well as the cumulative expenditures for year to date, and balances against each of the
revenue and expenditure appropriations.

This Q2 report is assessed against the 2025 Original budget.

The core economic classifications, as per the National Chart of Accounts (NCOA) refer to:

       Revenue – Economic Account Type 1
       Personnel Expenditure – Economic Sub-Account Type 21
       Overheads Expenditure - Economic Account Class 2202
       Capital Expenditure - Economic Sub-Account Type 23 / 32
       Other Expenditures (covering Loans, Grants and Contributions, Subsidies, Debt Service and Transfer
        Payments) - Economic Account Classes 2203-2209 as applicable

This report includes sections 3 and 4 detailed reports on Primary Healthcare and Basic Education
expenditures, respectively. Section Error! Reference source not found. identifies performance of climate
change responsive capital expenditure related to adaption and mitigation.
This Budget Implementation Report is produced by the Ministry of Planning and Budget Kano State in
collaboration with the Ministry of Finance Kano and published on the Kano State website.

1.B     Revenue Performance
The Recurrent Revenue including Federation Account Allocation Committee (FAAC) and the internally
Generated Revenue (IGR). In the Second Quarter (April – June) 2025, Kano State received
N130,562,492,452.19 as State FAAC Share which including Share of VAT, Statutory Allocation, Exchange Gain,
Ecological Fund, Ecological Fund, Electronic Money Transfer Levy (EMTL), Signature Bonus and other FAAC.
While Cumulative Performance of the FAAC from January to June 2025 is recorded N191,982,311,730.73 as
against the Approved Budget of N531,173,900,252.00 detecting 36.1% While on the side of Internally
Generated Revenue the performance is N30,931,337,495.07 for the quarter under review and the
cumulative collection for the Half year is N43,462,597,379.44 indicating 50.6% achievement . The total
amount state received by the state on Total Recurrent Revenue for the second quarter 2025               is
N161,493,829,947.26 while Cummulative performance from January – June 2025 is N235,444,909,110.17
recording 38.2% achievement.

1.C     Recurrent Expenditure Performance
While Recurrent Expenditure comprises of Personnel Cost, Overhead Cost and other Recurrent expenditure
which include settlement of liabilities both Domestic and Foreign liabilities,Grant and Subsidies ,etc .
The Performance of Recurrent Expenditure for the second quarter 2025 is 64,534,571,342.24 while cumulative
for the Half Year 2025 is N115,236,020,242.44 indicating 43.9% achievement. While Performance of Personnel
Cost for the quarter under review is N27,495,172,492.53 and Cumulative Perfromance is represented as
N56,934,416,879.57 representing 37.7% for the period January to June 2025.




Budget Implementation Report - 2025 Quarter 2                                                         Page | 3
 Kano State Government


On the other hand, the Overhead cost performance is N24,232,191,729.00 for the second quarter while the
sum of N37,816,627,744.03 was paid as Overhead for the period of January to June 2025. While other
Recurrent Expenditure that includes payment of Public debt both Domestic and Foreign is depicted as
N12,807,207,120.71for the second quarter while cumulative is recorded as N20,484,975,618.84 representing
88.5% against the Approved Budget of N23,154,385,548.08.

It is pertinent to inform that, on the course of the implementation of 2025 Approved Budget certain budget
codes in the area personnel cost and overhead cost were over performed. This necessitates initiation of Budget
formalization of the affected Codes or Budget line and it will be submitting to State House of Assembly for
budget amendment and supplementary.



1.D     Capital Expenditure Performance
The Capital Expenditure is finance through the Capital Development fund and Capital receipt which include
Grant, Aids, and Loan from both domestic and foreign.
Performance of Capital receipt for the second quarter 2025 is N6,975,753,249.78 while the cumulative is
N8,975,753,249.78 indicating 12.2% achievement. The poor performance is attributed to the non-disclosure
of the actual Capital receipt collected by various Projects in the State.
The performance of Capital Expenditure for the second quarter 2025 is N63,517,209,063.99 while cumulative
expenditure for the period of January to June 2025 is N90,790,594,069.05 representing 19.9% achievement.
This is attributed to the Non performance of the Capital receipts which is meant to complement
Government’s effort in providing developmental projects in the state. Therefore, Government initiated a
comprehensive strategy in tracking financial data related to Capital Receipt draw dawn by the State. This will
improve the performance of the Capital component in Budget Implementation.
Also, is important to note that, on the course of 2025 budget implementation certain Budget codes were
over performed and this informed the process of Budget formulisation and will be submitted to State House
of Assembly before third quarter 2025.

1.E     Conclusions
Conclusively, the aggregate Budget performance for the second quarter 2025 in the area of Total Revenue is
N168,469,583,197.04 while the cumulative for the half year performance is 267,055,162,359.95 indicating
37%. While aggregate expenditure for the second quarter is N128,051,780,406.23 and cumulative is
206,026,614,311.49 representing 28.6% achievement.
